## Reusable cleaning script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It defines the cleaning rules so both the existing files and the new extraction use **identical** logic.

```python
# scripts/clean_md.py
"""
Reusable cleaning rules for service-manual markdown pages.
Designed to be imported by extract_remaining.py and run standalone
over an existing ./md/ directory.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# Patterns that typically appear in Ford/GM-style workshop manuals
HEADER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^SECTION\s+\d+[-\s]\d+:\s*.+$",          # SECTION 204-04: Wheels and Tires
    r"^2014\s+F-150\s+Workshop\s+Manual\s*$",
    r"^Procedure\s+revision\s+date:\s*\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}\s*$",
]
FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^repair4less\s*$",
    r"^\d+\s*$",                                 # lone page number
]

# Hyphenation at end of line: word-\nword -> wordword
HYPHEN_LINE_BREAK = re.compile(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)")

# Collapse 3+ newlines to 2 (paragraph break)
MULTI_NEWLINE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")

# Strip leading/trailing whitespace on every line
TRAILING_WS = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def is_noise_line(line: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if the line is a running header/footer/watermark."""
    stripped = line.strip()
    if not stripped:
        return False
    for pat in HEADER_PATTERNS + FOOTER_PATTERNS:
        if re.match(pat, stripped, re.IGNORECASE):
            return True
    return False


def clean_text(raw: str) -> str:
    """Apply the full cleaning pipeline to a page's raw text."""
    lines = raw.splitlines()

    # 1. Remove header/footer noise lines
    lines = [ln for ln in lines if not is_noise_line(ln)]

    text = "\n".join(lines)

    # 2. Join hyphenated line breaks (e.g., "trans-\nmit" -> "transmit")
    text = HYPHEN_LINE_BREAK.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # 3. Normalize whitespace
    text = TRAILING_WS.sub("", text)
    text = MULTI_NEWLINE.sub("\n\n", text)

    # 4. Strip leading/trailing blank lines
    text = text.strip()

    return text


def clean_md_file(path: Path, dry_run: bool = False) -> str:
    """Clean a single markdown file in place (or return cleaned text if dry_run)."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    # Preserve YAML frontmatter if present
    frontmatter = ""
    body = raw
    if raw.startswith("---\n"):
        end = raw.find("\n---\n", 4)
        if end != -1:
            frontmatter = raw[: end + 5]  # include closing ---
            body = raw[end + 5 :]

    cleaned_body = clean_text(body)
    result = frontmatter + cleaned_body if frontmatter else cleaned_body

    if not dry_run:
        path.write_text(result, encoding="utf-8")
    return result


def clean_directory(md_dir: str | Path = "./md", dry_run: bool = False) -> list[Path]:
    """Clean every .md file in a directory."""
    md_path = Path(md_dir)
    files = sorted(md_path.glob("*.md"))
    for f in files:
        clean_md_file(f, dry_run=dry_run)
    return files
```

### How to use it on your existing files

```bash

python scripts/clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --dry-run

# Apply cleaning in place
python scripts/clean_md.py --md-dir ./md
```

```python
# scripts/extract_remaining.py
"""
Extract remaining PDF pages to ./md/ using PyMuPDF.
Resumable: skips any page that already has a markdown file.
Reuses the same cleaning rules as scripts/clean_md.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import fitz  # PyMuPDF

from clean_md import clean_text  # same cleaning rules

# Output naming convention: page_001.md, page_002.md, ...
PAGE_RE = re.compile(r"page_(\d+)\.md$")


def existing_pages(md_dir: Path) -> set[int]:
    """Return set of page numbers already extracted."""
    pages = set()
    for f in md_dir.glob("page_*.md"):
        m = PAGE_RE.search(f.name)
        if m:
            pages.add(int(m.group(1)))
    return pages


def extract_page(doc: fitz.Document, page_index: int) -> str:
    """Extract raw text from a PDF page (0-based index)."""
    page = doc[page_index]
    # Use "text" for flow; for table-heavy pages consider page.get_text("blocks")
    # and reconstruct markdown tables manually.
    return page.get_text("text")


def page_to_md(
    raw_text: str,
    page_number: int,
    pdf_name: str,
    section: Optional[str] = None,
    title: Optional[str] = None,
) -> str:
    """Wrap cleaned text in consistent YAML frontmatter + markdown body."""
    cleaned = clean_text(raw_text)

    # Attempt to grab the first non-empty line as a title if not provided
    if not title:
        for line in cleaned.splitlines():
            if line.strip():
                title = line.strip().replace("#", "").strip()
                break

    fm = f"""---
page: {page_number}
source_pdf: {pdf_name}
section: {section or ""}
title: {title or ""}
---

"""
    return fm + cleaned + "\n"


def extract_remaining(
    pdf_path: str,
    md_dir: str = "./md",
    start_page: int = 1,          # 1-based

## Reusable Cleaning Script (`scripts/clean_md.py`)

This script applies the same rules to existing files and will be reused by the extraction script. It is **configurable**—edit `HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS` once you see your actual files.

```python
# scripts/clean_md.py
"""
Reusable cleaning for service-manual markdown pages.
Run:  python scripts/clean_md.py
"""
import re
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURE THESE after inspecting your actual md files
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^\s*Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$",          # "Page 42 of 512"
    r"^\s*\d{1,3}\s*$",                         # standalone page number
    r"^\s*Service Manual\s*$",                  # doc title in header
    r"^\s*Predii\s*$",                          # company watermark
    r"^\s*A008\d{6}\s*$",                       # orphaned figure code (sample: A0086882)
]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Rejoin words split by hyphen + newline."""
    return re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1\2", text)

def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse spaces/tabs, limit blank lines, preserve paragraph breaks."""
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip()

def strip_headers_footers(text: str) -> str:
    """Remove lines that match header/footer patterns."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    kept = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if any(re.match(pat, stripped, re.IGNORECASE) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        kept.append(line)
    return "\n".join(kept)

def clean_markdown(text: str) -> str:
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = strip_headers_footers(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    return text

def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Directory {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}")
    for f in files:
        raw = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        cleaned = clean_markdown(raw)
        f.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"  cleaned -> {f.name}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Extraction Script for Remaining Pages (`scripts/extract_remaining.py`)

This uses **PyMuPDF (fitz)**, processes **only pages without an existing md file**, applies the *identical* cleaning rules, and writes to `./md/` with the same naming convention.

```python
# scripts/extract_remaining.py
"""
Extract remaining PDF pages to ./md/ using PyMuPDF + the same cleaning rules.
Resumable: skips pages that already have an md file.
Run:  python scripts/extract_remaining.py
"""
import fitz  # PyMuPDF
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURE
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PDF_PATH = Path("service_manual.pdf")   # <-- change to your actual PDF filename
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
MD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Import the cleaner from the sibling script
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from clean_md import clean_markdown  # noqa: E402

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def existing_pages() -> set[int]:
    """Return set of page numbers already extracted (based on filename)."""
    pages = set()
    for f in MD_DIR.glob("*.md"):
        # Adjust the regex below to match YOUR naming convention.
        # Examples supported: page_042.md, 042.md, p042.md, ch03_p042.md
        m = re.search(r"(\d{1,4})", f.stem)
        if m:
            pages.add(int(m.group(1)))
    return pages

import re  # noqa: E402  (moved down for readability)

def page_to_markdown(page: fitz.Page, page_num: int) -> str:
    """Extract text from a PDF page and wrap in a lightweight markdown header."""
    text = page.get_text("text")  # use "blocks" or "dict" if you need layout later
    md = f"---\npage: {page_num}\n---\n\n{text}\n"
    return clean_markdown(md)

def main():
    if not PDF_PATH.exists():
        print(f"PDF not found: {PDF_PATH}")
        return

    done = existing_pages()
    print(f"Already extracted: {sorted(done)}")

    doc = fitz.open(PDF_PATH)
    total = doc.page_count
    new_count = 0

    for i in range(total):
        page_num = i + 1  # 1-based
        if page_num in done:
            print(f"  skip  page {page_num}")
            continue

        page = doc[i]
        md = page_to_markdown(page, page_num)

        # Naming convention: page_042.md  (zero-padded to 3 digits)
        out_path = MD_DIR / f"page_{page_num:03d}.md"
        out_path.write_text(md, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"  wrote page {page_num} -> {out_path.name}")
        new_count += 1

    print(f"\nDone. Extracted {new_count} new page(s). Total pages in PDF: {total}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### 1. Inspection script (`inspect_md.py`)

Save this as `inspect_md.py` and run `python inspect_md.py`.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect existing markdown files in ./md/.
Reports: naming convention, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# Patterns commonly found in workshop-manual headers/footers
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:\s+.*", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"2014\s+F-?150\s+Workshop\s+Manual", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"Procedure\s+revision\s+date:\s+\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),  # lone page number
]

PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"(?:^|\n)\s*(?:#+\s*)?Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE)
HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", re.MULTILINE)
HYPHENATION_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")


def analyze_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    lines = text.splitlines()

    # Page number detection
    page_matches = PAGE_MARKER_RE.findall(text)
    headings = HEADING_RE.findall(text)

    # Header/footer detection: lines that match boilerplate
    hf_lines = [ln for ln in lines if any(p.search(ln) for p in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS)]

    # Hyphenation / broken words across line breaks
    hyphen_count = len(HYPHENATION_RE.findall(text))

    # Table heuristics: lines with many pipes but not a clean markdown table
    table_like = [ln for ln in lines if ln.count("|") >= 2 and not ln.strip().startswith("|")]

    # Repeated lines across the file (possible running header/footer)
    line_counts = Counter(lines)
    repeated = {ln: c for ln, c in line_counts.items() if c > 1 and ln.strip()}

    return {
        "file": path.name,
        "chars": len(text),
        "lines": len(lines),
        "pages_found": page_matches,
        "headings": headings,
        "header_footer_lines": hf_lines,
        "hyphenation_issues": hyphen_count,
        "table_like_lines": len(table_like),
        "repeated_lines": repeated,
        "first_line": lines[0].strip() if lines else "",
        "last_line": lines[-1].strip() if lines else "",
    }


def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Directory {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown file(s) in {MD_DIR}\n")
    print("=" * 100)

    all_pages = []
    all_headings = []
    total_hyphens = 0
    total_table_like = 0
    all_repeated = Counter()

    for f in files:
        r = analyze_file(f)
        all_pages.extend(r["pages_found"])
        all_headings.extend(r["headings"])
        total_hyphens += r["hyphenation_issues"]
        total_table_like += r["table_like_lines"]
        all_repeated.update(r["repeated_lines"])

        print(f"\nFILE: {r['file']}")
        print(f"  Size: {r['chars']} chars, {r['lines']} lines")
        print(f"  Page markers found: {r['pages_found'] if r['pages_found'] else 'NONE'}")
        print(f"  Headings: {r['headings'] if r['headings'] else 'NONE'}")
        print(f"  First line: {r['first_line'][:80]}")
        print(f"  Last line:  {r['last_line'][:80]}")
        if r["header_footer_lines"]:
            print(f"  [ISSUE] Header/footer-like lines ({len(r['header_footer_lines'])}):")
            for ln in r["header_footer_lines"][:5]:
                print(f"         - {ln[:80]}")
        if r["hyphenation_issues"]:
            print(f"  [ISSUE] Hyphenation/broken-word issues: {r['hyphenation_issues']}")
        if r["table_like_lines"]:
            print(f"  [ISSUE] Possible broken table lines: {r['table_like_lines']}")
        if r["repeated_lines"]:
            print(f"  [ISSUE] Repeated lines (possible running header/footer):")
            for ln, c in list(r["repeated_lines"].items())[:5]:
                print(f"         - '{ln[:60]}' x{c}")

    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("AGGREGATE FINDINGS")
    print("=" * 100)
    print(f"Total files: {len(files)}")
    print(f"Unique page markers found: {sorted(set(all_pages))}")
    print(f"Total headings detected: {len(all_headings)}")
    print(f"Total hyphenation issues: {total_hyphens}")
    print(f"Total possible broken table lines: {total_table_like}")
    if all_repeated:
        print("Most common repeated lines (possible headers/footers):")
        for ln, c in all_repeated.most_common(10):
            print(f"  {c:3d}x  {ln[:80]}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### 2. Reusable cleaning script (`clean_md.py`)

Save this as `clean_md.py`. It reads every `*.md` in `./md/`, applies the same rules, and overwrites them (or writes to `./md_clean/` if you prefer). It is designed so `extract_remaining.py` can import the same `clean_text()` function.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable cleaning script for extracted workshop-manual markdown.
Applies identical rules to existing files and new PyMuPDF extractions.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# Boilerplate to strip from headers/footers (tune after inspection)
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*SECTION\s+\d+-\d+:\s+.*\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*2014\s+F-?150\s+Workshop\s+Manual\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*Procedure\s+revision\s+date:\s+\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,3}\s*$"),  # standalone page number
]

PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"(?:^|\n)\s*(?:#+\s*)?Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE)
HYPHENATION_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")
MULTI_BLANK_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def clean_text(raw: str, source_page: int | None = None) -> str:
    """Clean a single page's extracted text and return markdown."""
    text = raw

    # 1. Remove running headers / footers line-by-line
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines = []
    for ln in lines:
        if any(p.search(ln) for p in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        cleaned_lines.append(ln)
    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # 2. Fix hyphenation / broken words across line breaks
    text = HYPHENATION_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # 3. Normalize whitespace
    text = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", text)
    text = MULTI_BLANK_RE.sub("\n\n", text)

    # 4. Ensure an explicit page marker at the top if we know it
    if source_page is not None:
        if not PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(text):
            text = f"<!-- Page {source_page} -->\n\n{text}"

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_directory(in_dir: Path = MD_DIR, out_dir: Path | None = None):
    out_dir = out_dir or in_dir
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    for path in sorted(in_dir.glob("*.md")):
        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        # Try to infer page number from existing marker or filename
        m = PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(raw)
        page = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
        if page is None:
            m2 = re.search(r"(\d+)", path.stem)
            page = int(m2.group(1)) if m2 else None

        cleaned = clean_text(raw, source_page=page)
        (out_dir / path.name).write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"Cleaned: {path.name} (page={page})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    clean_directory()
```

- Skips any page that already has `./md/page_XXX.md`.
- Extracts text, applies the

## Step 1: Inspect & Clean Existing Markdown

Save this as `scripts/inspect_and_clean.py`. It reports naming, format, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems, then applies a reusable cleaning pass.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect existing ./md/ files and apply reusable cleaning.
Run: python scripts/inspect_and_clean.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
CLEAN_DIR = Path("./md_clean")  # cleaned output; you can overwrite ./md/ later

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. INSPECTION
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def inspect_md_files(md_dir: Path) -> List[Dict]:
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    report = []
    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()
        first_lines = lines[:5]
        last_lines = lines[-5:]

        # Heuristics
        has_page_header = any(re.search(r"page\s+\d+", l, re.I) for l in first_lines + last_lines)
        has_footer = any(re.search(r"(repair4less|copyright|©|manual|version)", l, re.I) for l in last_lines)
        has_heading = any(l.startswith("#") for l in lines)
        broken_table = bool(re.search(r"\|.*\|.*\|", text)) and text.count("|") % 2 != 0
        hyphenation = bool(re.search(r"-\n\w", text))  # word split across lines
        non_ascii = len(re.findall(r"[^\x00-\x7F]+", text))

        report.append({
            "file": f.name,
            "size_bytes": f.stat().st_size,
            "lines": len(lines),
            "has_page_header": has_page_header,
            "has_footer": has_footer,
            "has_heading": has_heading,
            "possible_broken_table": broken_table,
            "possible_hyphenation": hyphenation,
            "non_ascii_chars": non_ascii,
            "first_line": first_lines[0] if first_lines else "",
            "last_line": last_lines[-1] if last_lines else "",
        })
    return report

def print_inspection(report: List[Dict]):
    print(f"{'File':<25} {'Lines':>6} {'PageHdr':>8} {'Footer':>8} {'Heading':>8} {'Table?':>7} {'Hyp?':>5} {'NonASCII':>9}")
    print("-" * 90)
    for r in report:
        print(f"{r['file']:<25} {r['lines']:>6} {str(r['has_page_header']):>8} {str(r['has_footer']):>8} "
              f"{str(r['has_heading']):>8} {str(r['possible_broken_table']):>7} {str(r['possible_hyphenation']):>5} "
              f"{r['non_ascii_chars']:>9}")
    print("\nSample first/last lines:")
    for r in report[:5]:
        print(f"  {r['file']}:")
        print(f"    START: {r['first_line'][:100]}")
        print(f"    END:   {r['last_line'][:100]}")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. REUSABLE CLEANING RULES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    # Normalize line endings
    text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")

    # Remove common service-manual headers/footers (tune these after inspection)
    text = re.sub(r"(?m)^\s*(repair4less|page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+|©\s*\d{4}|all rights reserved)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)

    # Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "trans-\nmission" -> "transmission"
    text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1\2", text)

    # Collapse 3+ newlines to 2 (paragraph separation)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # Strip trailing whitespace on each line
    text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.splitlines())

    # Remove leading/trailing blank lines
    text = text.strip()
    return text

def clean_and_save(md_dir: Path, out_dir: Path):
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    summary = []
    for f in files:
        raw = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        cleaned = clean_text(raw)
        out_path = out_dir / f.name
        out_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        summary.append({
            "file": f.name,
            "raw_len": len(raw),
            "clean_len": len(cleaned),
            "removed_chars": len(raw) - len(cleaned),
        })
    return summary

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Create it and add your .md files.")
        raise SystemExit(1)

    report = inspect_md_files(MD_DIR)
    print_inspection(report)

    # Save inspection report
    Path("inspection_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("\nSaved inspection_report.json")

    # Ask before cleaning
    ans = input("\nApply cleaning and write to ./md_clean/? (y/N): ").strip().lower()
    if ans == "y":
        summary = clean_and_save(MD_DIR, CLEAN_DIR)
        print("\nCleaning summary:")
        for s in summary:
            print(f"  {s['file']}: {s['raw_len']} -> {s['clean_len']} chars (removed {s['removed_chars']})")
        print(f"\nCleaned files written to {CLEAN_DIR}/")
```

### `scripts/extract_remaining.py` (Step 2)
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 2: Extract ONLY remaining pages with PyMuPDF, using same cleaning rules
as Step 1, output same markdown format, resumable (skips existing files).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import fitz  # PyMuPDF
import re
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# Add repo root to path so we can import shared cleaning
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from pipeline.cleaning import clean_text  # see pipeline/cleaning.py below

PDF_PATH = Path("pdf/service_manual.pdf")   # <-- update path
MD_DIR = Path("md_clean")                   # write into cleaned dir
START_PAGE = 0                              # 0-indexed; set to Y+1 from your state
END_PAGE = None                             # None = last page

def page_to_markdown(page: fitz.Page, page_num: int) -> str:
    """Extract page text as markdown with a YAML-style header for metadata."""
    text = page.get_text("text")
    # Very light structure detection: lines that look like headings
    lines = text.splitlines()
    md_lines = [f"---\npage: {page_num + 1}\n---\n"]
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped:
            md_lines.append("")
            continue
        # Heuristic: short, title-case lines become headings
        if len(stripped) < 60 and stripped.istitle() and not stripped.endswith("."):
            md_lines.append(f"## {stripped}")
        else:
            md_lines.append(stripped)
    return "\n".join(md_lines)

def main():
    if not PDF_PATH.exists():
        print(f"PDF not found: {PDF_PATH}")
        return

    MD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc = fitz.open(str(PDF_PATH))
    total = doc.page_count
    end = END_PAGE if END_PAGE is not None else total - 1

    print(f"PDF has {total} pages. Extracting pages {START_PAGE} to {end} (0-indexed).")

    for i in range(START_PAGE, end + 1):
        out_name = f"page_{i+1:03d}.md"
        out_path = MD_DIR / out_name
        if out_path.exists():
            print(f"  Skip {out_name} (already exists)")
            continue
        page = doc[i]
        md = page_to_markdown(page, i)
        md = clean_text(md)
        out_path.write_text(md, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"  Wrote {out_name}")

    print("Done.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### 1.1 Inspection script (`inspect_md.py`)

Save this as `inspect_md.py` and run it from the project root:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect existing ./md/ files: naming, page markers, headings, cleaning issues."""
import re
from pathlib import Path

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

PAGE_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"<!--\s*page\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*-->", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^#+\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^(\d{1,4})\s*$", re.M),  # lone number line
]

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Directory {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist yet.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()

        # --- Page number detection ---
        page_found = None
        for pat in PAGE_PATTERNS:
            m = pat.search(text)
            if m:
                page_found = m.group(1)
                break

        # --- Headings ---
        headings = [l.strip() for l in lines if l.strip().startswith("#")]

        # --- Cleaning-problem signals ---
        form_feeds = text.count("\f")
        hyphen_splits = re.findall(r"\w+-\n\w+", text)
        lone_dashes = [l for l in lines if re.fullmatch(r"[-_=\s]{3,}", l.strip())]
        lone_numbers = [l for l in lines if re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,4}", l.strip())]

        print(f"--- {f.name} ---")
        print(f"  Size: {f.stat().st_size} bytes | Lines: {len(lines)}")
        print(f"  Page marker: {page_found if page_found else 'NOT FOUND'}")
        print(f"  First line : {lines[0][:90] if lines else ''}")
        print(f"  Last line  : {lines[-1][:90] if lines else ''}")
        print(f"  Headings ({len(headings)}): {headings[:4]}")
        print(f"  Form feeds: {form_feeds}")
        print(f"  Hyphenation splits: {len(hyphen_splits)} -> {hyphen_splits[:5]}")
        print(f"  Lone dash/separator lines: {len(lone_dashes)}")
        print(f"  Lone number lines (possible header/footer page #): {len(lone_numbers)}")
        print()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

## Step 1a — Inspection Script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` in your project root and run:

```bash
python inspect_md.py
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
inspect_md.py
Inspects ./md/ extracted markdown files and reports:
- naming convention
- page number / heading preservation
- common cleaning problems (headers/footers, broken tables, hyphenation, etc.)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

PAGE_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^#?\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE),
    re.compile(r"^---+\s*Page\s+(\d+)\s*---+", re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE),
    re.compile(r"^(\d{1,4})\s*$", re.MULTILINE),  # bare number on its own line
]

HEADING_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^\s{0,3}#{1,6}\s+.+", re.MULTILINE)
TABLE_LINE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^\s*\|.*\|\s*$")
HYPHENATION_PATTERN = re.compile(r"-\s*$")  # line ends with hyphen
FORM_FEED = "\f"
NULL_BYTE = "\x00"


def detect_page_number(text: str, fname: str) -> Tuple[str | None, str]:
    """Try to find a page number in text or filename."""
    for pat in PAGE_PATTERNS:
        m = pat.search(text)
        if m:
            return m.group(1), "text"
    # fallback: parse from filename like page_042.md or 042.md
    m = re.search(r"(\d{1,4})", fname.stem)
    if m:
        return m.group(1), "filename"
    return None, "none"


def count_headings(text: str) -> int:
    return len(HEADING_PATTERN.findall(text))


def count_table_lines(text: str) -> int:
    return len(TABLE_LINE_PATTERN.findall(text))


def find_repeated_lines(files: List[Path], top_n: int = 5) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    """Find first/last lines that appear in many files (likely headers/footers)."""
    first_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    last_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for f in files:
        lines = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
        if lines:
            first_counter[lines[0].strip()] += 1
            last_counter[lines[-1].strip()] += 1
    repeated = []
    for line, cnt in first_counter.most_common(top_n):
        if cnt > 1 and line:
            repeated.append((f"FIRST: {line}", cnt))
    for line, cnt in last_counter.most_common(top_n):
        if cnt > 1 and line:
            repeated.append((f"LAST : {line}", cnt))
    return repeated


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main inspection
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist.")
        return

    md_files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No .md files found in {MD_DIR.resolve()}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")
    print("=" * 100)
    print(f"{'File':<30} {'Pages':<8} {'Headings':<10} {'TableLines':<12} {'Chars':<8} {'Issues'}")
    print("=" * 100)

    all_issues: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    total_chars = 0

    for f in md_files:
        raw = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        total_chars += len(raw)

        page_num, src = detect_page_number(raw, f)
        headings = count_headings(raw)
        table_lines = count_table_lines(raw)

        issues: List[str] = []

        # 1. Page number missing
        if page_num is None:
            issues.append("no page number")

        # 2. Hyphenation across line breaks
        hyphens = len(HYPHENATION_PATTERN.findall(raw))
        if hyphens > 0:
            issues.append(f"{hyphens} hyphenated line-breaks")

        # 3. Form feed / null bytes
        if FORM_FEED in raw:
            issues.append("contains form-feed (\\f)")
        if NULL_BYTE in raw:
            issues.append("contains null bytes")

        # 4. Broken / inconsistent tables (naive: table lines with varying pipe counts)
        table_lines_list = TABLE_LINE_PATTERN.findall(raw)
        if table_lines_list:
            pipe_counts = [line.count("|") for line in table_lines_list]
            if len(set(pipe_counts)) > 1:
                issues.append(f"inconsistent table pipes ({min(pipe_counts)}-{max(pipe_counts)})")

        # 5. Very short file (possible extraction failure)
        if len(raw.strip()) < 50:
            issues.append("suspiciously short")

        all_issues[f.name] = issues

        print(
            f"{f.name:<30} {str(page_num):<8} {headings:<10} {table_lines:<12} {len(raw):<8} "
            f"{'; '.join(issues) if issues else 'clean'}"
        )

    print("=" * 100)
    print(f"\nTotal characters: {total_chars:,}")
    print(f"Files with issues: {sum(1 for v in all_issues.values() if v)} / {len(md_files)}")

    # Repeated first/last lines (header/footer candidates)
    repeated = find_repeated_lines(md_files)
    if repeated:
        print("\n--- Repeated first/last lines (possible headers/footers) ---")
        for line, cnt in repeated:
            print(f"  [{cnt} files] {line}")

    # Sample of files with issues
    print("\n--- First 5 files with issues (detail) ---")
    shown = 0
    for f in md_files:
        if all_issues[f.name]:
            print(f"\n### {f.name}")
            print(all_issues[f.name])
            shown += 1
            if shown >= 5:
                break


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Step 1b — Cleaning Script (template)

Once you share the inspection output, I will finalize this script. For now, here is the **reusable structure** it will follow. Save as `clean_md.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
clean_md.py
Reusable cleaning for extracted service-manual markdown.
Rules are configurable; run with --dry-run first.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration – EDIT THESE TO MATCH YOUR MANUAL
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^repair4less\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^Page \d+ of \d+$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^© .+ All rights reserved\.\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
]

# If your files already contain a page marker, set this to the regex that finds it.
PAGE_MARKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^#?\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleaning functions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def strip_headers_footers(text: str) -> str:
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if any(pat.fullmatch(stripped) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        cleaned.append(line)
    return "\n".join(cleaned)


def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Join words broken across lines by a trailing hyphen."""
    return re.sub(r"-\n(\s*)", r"\1", text)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    # Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2; strip trailing spaces per line
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+$", "", text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip() + "\n"


def ensure_page_marker(text: str, page_num: int | None) -> str:
    if page_num is None:
        return text
    if PAGE_MARKER_PATTERN.search(text):
        return text  # already present
    return f"---\nPage {page_num}\n---\n\n{text}"


def clean_file(
    src: Path,
    dst: Path | None = None,
    page_num: int | None = None,
    dry_run: bool = False,
) -> str:
    raw = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    text = strip_headers_footers(raw)
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    text = ensure_page_marker(text, page_num)

    if dry_run:
        return text

    out = dst or src
    out.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    return text


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted markdown files.")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Directory with .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", default=None, help="Output directory (default: in-place)")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print cleaned text only")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir) if args.out_dir else None
    if out_dir:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    for f in sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md")):
        m = re.search(r"(\d{1,4})", f.stem)
        page_num = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
        clean_file(f, out_dir / f.name if out_dir else None, page_num, dry_run=args.dry_run)
        print(f"{'[dry-run] ' if args.dry_run else ''}cleaned: {f.name}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

### Script 1: Inspect existing `./md/` files

Save as `inspect_md.py` and run from the project root:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect existing markdown files in ./md/ and report format/issues."""
import re
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")

    if not files:
        return

    # Show naming pattern sample
    stems = [f.stem for f in files[:10]]
    print("Sample filenames:", stems)
    print()

    # Aggregate stats
    total_chars = 0
    total_lines = 0
    heading_counter = Counter()
    issue_counter = Counter()
    page_patterns = set()

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        total_chars += len(text)
        total_lines += len(text.splitlines())

        # Headings
        for m in re.finditer(r'^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)', text, re.MULTILINE):
            heading_counter[(len(m.group(1)), m.group(2)[:60])] += 1

        # Page-number-like patterns
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?:page\s+)?(\d{1,4})\b', text, re.IGNORECASE):
            page_patterns.add(m.group(1))

        # Common cleaning problems
        if re.search(r'repair4less', text, re.IGNORECASE):
            issue_counter["watermark: repair4less"] += 1
        if re.search(r'^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$', text, re.MULTILINE):
            issue_counter["standalone page number line"] += 1
        if re.search(r'(\w)-\n(\w)', text):
            issue_counter["hyphenation line break"] += 1
        if re.search(r'\n{4,}', text):
            issue_counter["excessive blank lines"] += 1
        if re.search(r'[^\x00-\x7F]+', text):
            issue_counter["non-ascii characters"] += 1

    print(f"Total chars: {total_chars:,} | Total lines: {total_lines:,}")
    print(f"Avg chars/file: {total_chars//len(files):,}\n")

    print("Top heading patterns (level, text):")
    for (level, text), count in heading_counter.most_common(10):
        print(f"  {'#'*level} {text}  ({count}x)")

    print("\nDetected issues (file count):")
    for issue, count in issue_counter.most_common():
        print(f"  {count}x  {issue}")

    print(f"\nDistinct page-number-like tokens found: {sorted(page_patterns)[:20]}")

    # Print first file as a format sample
    print("\n--- Sample file content (first 800 chars) ---")
    sample = files[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    print(sample[:800])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

    """Try to pull a page number from the filename."""

    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

def clean_text(text: str, page_num: int | None = None) -> str:

    # 2. Remove common header/footer watermarks

```markdown
# Driveline System

## DESCRIPTION AND OPERATION

The driveline consists of the following:

- Front drive axle
- Rear drive axle
- Front axle halfshafts
- Front driveshaft
- Rear driveshaft
- Center support bearings
- CV joints
- U-joints and flanges
- Flex couplers

The source of the drivetrain power is generated by the engine and delivered to the transmission. The driveline transfers the engine torque through the driveshaft to the axle. The rear driveshaft is connected to the output shaft of the transmission or transfer case and to the rear axle. The front driveshaft is connected to the transfer case and to the front axle. Slip yokes are used to allow for any changes to the length of the driveshaft. Changes in length are self-adjusted up in the driveshaft slip mechanism. The engine torque enters the axle through the drive pinion, which rotates the ring gear. The ring gear is mounted to the differential case, which contains the gears that transmit power to the rear axle shafts or front halfshafts. These shafts and halfshafts rotate the drive wheels. The Electronic Locking Differential (ELD) rear axle assembly, except for the differential case and its internal components, is identical to the conventional rear axle. The ELD employs an electronically operated magnetic coil to engage the unit. The Traction-Lok rear axle assembly, except for the differential case and its internal components, is identical to the conventional rear axle. The Traction-Lok employs 2 multi-disc differential clutch packs to control differential action. The mounting distance of the differential side gear is controlled by plates on each side, friction and steel. Also included is a steel selective shim to control the position of the differential side gear, and a spring which provides initial preload to the clutch pack. The steel clutch plates are splined to the axle shafts. The friction plates are contained in the case by matching slots, thus providing continuous engagement of the differential clutch packs. The Torsen® front differential case houses the parallel axis planetary helical side gears to which the axle shafts are splined. The engine angle is built into the engine mounts. If the engine angle is out of specification, the engine mounts must be inspected for damage.

## Reusable Cleaning Script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It is designed to be run against your existing `./md/` folder and later against newly extracted pages so the whole manual is consistent.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
clean_md.py
Reusable cleaning script for Predii service-manual markdown pages.

Rules:
- Strip running headers/footers (manual title, revision date, section banner).
- Preserve page markers if present (e.g., "--- Page 42 ---").
- Join hyphenated line breaks (e.g., "drivetrain-\npower" -> "drivetrain power").
- Normalize whitespace and blank lines.
- Preserve markdown headings, lists, and tables.
- Skip files that already look clean (idempotent).
"""

import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration: adjust these regexes to match your manual's running headers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
RUNNING_HEADER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^2014 F-150 Workshop Manual\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^Procedure revision date:\s*\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4}\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^SECTION \d+-\d+:\s+.+—\s+.+\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),  # e.g. SECTION 205-00: ...
]

PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"^--- Page (\d+) ---\s*$")
HYPHEN_LINE_BREAK_RE = re.compile(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)")
MULTIPLE_BLANKS_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines = []

    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()

        # Drop running header/footer lines
        if any(pat.match(stripped) for pat in RUNNING_HEADER_PATTERNS):
            continue

        cleaned_lines.append(line)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # Join hyphenated words split across lines
    text = HYPHEN_LINE_BREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)

    # Normalize whitespace
    text = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", text)
    text = MULTIPLE_BLANKS_RE.sub("\n\n", text)

    return text.strip() + "\n"


def process_file(path: Path, dry_run: bool = False) -> str:
    original = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_text(original)

    if cleaned == original:
        return "unchanged"

    if not dry_run:
        path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
    return "cleaned"


def main():
    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted markdown pages.")
    parser.add_argument("input_dir", type=Path, help="Directory containing .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Show changes without writing")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.input_dir.exists():
        print(f"Directory not found: {args.input_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    md_files = sorted(args.input_dir.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(md_files)} markdown files in {args.input_dir}")

    for f in md_files:
        status = process_file(f, dry_run=args.dry_run)
        print(f"[{status}] {f.name}")

    if args.dry_run:
        print("\nDry run complete. Remove --dry-run to apply changes.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### Usage

```bash
# Inspect what would change (no files modified)
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md --dry-run

# Apply cleaning
python scripts/clean_md.py ./md
```
